### Verified bibliography and organism-name accounting

evidence: supported. Targets: CultureMech:007101.

Added PMID:9352926 with DOI alias, verified title/authors/year, source-discovery provenance and archived abstract links. Scientific names and exact/synonym/qualified parent mappings are recorded without asserting growth.

### Exact formulation remains unresolved

quantity: concern. Targets: CultureMech:007101.

Citation relevance is established, but exact ingredient forms, amounts, preparation and assay scope are not fully established. Differences and access gaps remain citation notes, not a resolved variant or corrected parent recipe.

Nakano: inherited Spizizen base, chemical-form notation, glucose conversion, amino-acid mixture basis and generic filtration need primary-source reconciliation. Xu: abstract-only access leaves exact formulation and strain unresolved. No model prediction or database growth row is promoted to cultivation evidence.

### formulation-gaps: Exact paper-to-formulation reconciliation remains incomplete

major / open / confirmed; issue key: mediadb-77-124-paper-formulation-reconciliation.

The verified papers are relevant, but the accessible source text does not certify the imported recipes. Nakano has unresolved base/form/unit/preparation questions; Xu has unresolved methods and strain scope. This finding denotes an evidence gap, not a conclusion that all 602 amounts are wrong.

### yeast-category: Yeast-source formulations retain bacterial classification

major / open / confirmed; issue key: mediadb-source124-yeast-bacterial-category.

The 32 source 124 normalized owners have category bacterial despite the verified paper/taxon yeast identity. No migration or category correction was silently bundled with these citations.

## Recommended Actions And Acceptance Checks

### reconcile-formulations

Obtain and inspect original methods, base recipes and supplements; represent supported differences in existing or new inline variants without certifying unsupported parent amounts.

- Every proposed concentration change has DOI/PURL and exact source snippet with units and stock/final basis.
- Resolve exact study formulation and strain before adding growth; separate modeled, negative and transient outcomes.
- Preserve scoped evidence and all unrelated parent content; run native validators.

### correct-yeast-classification

Review source 124 recipe category and path ownership, then perform a guarded category/link migration if supported.

- Classification agrees with the verified source/taxon without changing permanent IDs.
- Any path changes preserve references and parent/child links and pass exact-path validation.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- This is a focused, partial citation review, not a full-record or corpus-wide scientific pass.
- Nakano PDF text was inspected; no local full PDF or visual figure/unit verification is claimed. Xu access was abstract-only.
- All 602 ingredient instances were preserved, not recertified. No positive organism-medium assertion was added.
- Bounded NCBI name searches do not prove taxon absence; parent mappings remain explicit and are not exact strain identities.
- Required merge verification was deliberately interrupted after its reproduced name conflicts; remaining diagnostics incomplete. Freshness failed separately. No generated outputs published.
- Separate archival follow-up recovered four publisher-deposited abstracts and a full Carini 2014 candidate paper; it does not expand this 44-record scientific review or cure all archive gaps.
- Baseline checks covered schema and strict validation. Post checks additionally covered terms and references; their results are not retrospective baseline checks.
- The existing KGX inline-variant scope limitation remains relevant for future growth edits, but this batch adds no affected growth edge.
- The whole-corpus citation-exhaustion objective remains unproven; the current MediaDB candidate queue still contains unreviewed source-owner links.

  title: Exact paper-to-formulation reconciliation remains incomplete
  description: The verified papers are relevant, but the accessible source text does
    not certify the imported recipes. Nakano has unresolved base/form/unit/preparation
    questions; Xu has unresolved methods and strain scope. This finding denotes an
    evidence gap, not a conclusion that all 602 amounts are wrong.

limitations:
- This is a focused, partial citation review, not a full-record or corpus-wide scientific
  pass.
- Nakano PDF text was inspected; no local full PDF or visual figure/unit verification
  is claimed. Xu access was abstract-only.
- All 602 ingredient instances were preserved, not recertified. No positive organism-medium
  assertion was added.
- Bounded NCBI name searches do not prove taxon absence; parent mappings remain explicit
  and are not exact strain identities.
- Required merge verification was deliberately interrupted after its reproduced name
  conflicts; remaining diagnostics incomplete. Freshness failed separately. No generated
  outputs published.
- Separate archival follow-up recovered four publisher-deposited abstracts and a full
  Carini 2014 candidate paper; it does not expand this 44-record scientific review
  or cure all archive gaps.
- Baseline checks covered schema and strict validation. Post checks additionally covered
  terms and references; their results are not retrospective baseline checks.
- The existing KGX inline-variant scope limitation remains relevant for future growth
  edits, but this batch adds no affected growth edge.
- The whole-corpus citation-exhaustion objective remains unproven; the current MediaDB
  candidate queue still contains unreviewed source-owner links.
